=== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\DigitalFarmers_2331515\.agents\skills\library-skills\SKILL.md ===
0001: ---
0002: name: library-skills
0003: description: Use Library Skills to discover, install, refresh, repair, check, and manage agent skills from installed packages.
0004: ---
0005: 
0006: # Library Skills
0007: 
0008: Use this skill when a project might benefit from agent skills bundled by its installed packages, or when existing Library Skills-managed symlinks are stale, broken, orphaned, or need to be checked.
0009: 
0010: Run commands from the project root.
0011: 
0012: Agents bundle their own skills by including an `.agents/skills` directory. More details in [Library Skills](https://library-skills.io).
0013: 
0014: ## First-Time Setup
0015: 
0016: - Make sure project dependencies are installed first, for example with `uv sync` for Python projects or `npm install` / `bun install` for Node.js projects.
0017: - Run `uvx library-skills` or `npx library-skills` to discover skills bundled by the installed packages and install selected skills interactively.
0018: - Use `uvx library-skills --all` or `npx library-skills --all` only when all newly discovered skills should be installed without selecting individual skills.
0019: - Use `uvx library-skills --tool-skill` or `npx library-skills --tool-skill` to copy this Library Skills tool skill into the project so future agents know how to discover, install, update, repair, and check skills.
0020: 
0021: ## Commands
0022: 
0023: - Run `uvx library-skills` or `npx library-skills` to discover package-provided skills, install selected new skills, and reconcile existing managed symlinks.
0024: - Run `uvx library-skills list` or `npx library-skills list` to inspect discovered and installed skills.
0025: - Run `uvx library-skills list --json` or `npx library-skills list --json` for machine-readable installed status.
0026: - Run `uvx library-skills scan --json` or `npx library-skills scan --json` for discovery-only automation.
0027: - Run `uvx library-skills --check` or `npx library-skills --check` to validate managed skill symlink state without changing files.
0028: - Run `uvx library-skills --yes` or `npx library-skills --yes` to repair stale managed symlinks and remove orphaned managed symlinks non-interactively.
0029: - Add `--claude` when `.claude/skills` should also be managed.
0030: - Add `--skill NAME` to install a specific discovered skill by name.
0031: 
0032: ## Safety
0033: 
0034: - Prefer rerunning `library-skills` over editing managed symlinks manually.
0035: - If installed skill symlinks are broken, dependencies may not be installed yet. Try the project's normal install command first, such as `uv sync`, `npm install`, or `bun install`, then rerun `library-skills`.
0036: - Do not delete or overwrite hand-authored skill directories.
0037: - Library Skills only removes managed symlinks. It should not remove copied or hand-authored skill directories.

0001: ---
0002: name: library-skills
0003: description: Use Library Skills to discover, install, refresh, repair, check, and manage agent skills from installed packages.
0004: ---
0005: 
0006: # Library Skills
0007: 
0008: Use this skill when a project might benefit from agent skills bundled by its installed packages, or when existing Library Skills-managed symlinks are stale, broken, orphaned, or need to be checked.
0009: 
0010: Run commands from the project root.
0011: 
0012: Agents bundle their own skills by including an `.agents/skills` directory. More details in [Library Skills](https://library-skills.io).
0013: 
0014: ## First-Time Setup
0015: 
0016: - Make sure project dependencies are installed first, for example with `uv sync` for Python projects or `npm install` / `bun install` for Node.js projects.
0017: - Run `uvx library-skills` or `npx library-skills` to discover skills bundled by the installed packages and install selected skills interactively.
0018: - Use `uvx library-skills --all` or `npx library-skills --all` only when all newly discovered skills should be installed without selecting individual skills.
0019: - Use `uvx library-skills --tool-skill` or `npx library-skills --tool-skill` to copy this Library Skills tool skill into the project so future agents know how to discover, install, update, repair, and check skills.
0020: 
0021: ## Commands
0022: 
0023: - Run `uvx library-skills` or `npx library-skills` to discover package-provided skills, install selected new skills, and reconcile existing managed symlinks.
0024: - Run `uvx library-skills list` or `npx library-skills list` to inspect discovered and installed skills.
0025: - Run `uvx library-skills list --json` or `npx library-skills list --json` for machine-readable installed status.
0026: - Run `uvx library-skills scan --json` or `npx library-skills scan --json` for discovery-only automation.
0027: - Run `uvx library-skills --check` or `npx library-skills --check` to validate managed skill symlink state without changing files.
0028: - Run `uvx library-skills --yes` or `npx library-skills --yes` to repair stale managed symlinks and remove orphaned managed symlinks non-interactively.
0029: - Add `--claude` when `.claude/skills` should also be managed.
0030: - Add `--skill NAME` to install a specific discovered skill by name.
0031: 
0032: ## Safety
0033: 
0034: - Prefer rerunning `library-skills` over editing managed symlinks manually.
0035: - If installed skill symlinks are broken, dependencies may not be installed yet. Try the project's normal install command first, such as `uv sync`, `npm install`, or `bun install`, then rerun `library-skills`.
0036: - Do not delete or overwrite hand-authored skill directories.
0037: - Library Skills only removes managed symlinks. It should not remove copied or hand-authored skill directories.

0001: import os
0002: from pathlib import Path
0003: from typing import Any
0004: from urllib.parse import urlencode
0005: 
0006: from dotenv import load_dotenv
0007: from fastapi import HTTPException
0008: import httpx
0009: 
0010: load_dotenv(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / ".env")
0011: 
0012: _RAW_URL = (os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL") or "").strip().rstrip("/")
0013: if _RAW_URL.endswith("/rest/v1"):
0014:     _RAW_URL = _RAW_URL[: -len("/rest/v1")]
0015: 
0016: SUPABASE_URL = _RAW_URL
0017: SUPABASE_KEY = (os.getenv("SUPABASE_KEY") or "").strip()
0018: 
0019: 
0020: def _headers() -> dict[str, str]:
0021:     if not SUPABASE_URL or not SUPABASE_KEY:
0022:         raise HTTPException(
0023:             status_code=500,
0024:             detail="Supabase URL or API key is missing in backend/.env",
0025:         )
0026:     return {
0027:         "apikey": SUPABASE_KEY,
0028:         "Authorization": f"Bearer {SUPABASE_KEY}",
0029:         "Content-Type": "application/json",
0030:         "Prefer": "return=representation",
0031:     }
0032: 
0033: 
0034: def _build_url(table: str, filters: dict[str, Any] | str | None = None) -> str:
0035:     clean_table = table.strip().lstrip("/")
0036:     url = f"{SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/{clean_table}"
0037:     if isinstance(filters, str):
0038:         q = filters.strip().lstrip("?")
0039:         if q:
0040:             url = f"{url}?{q}"
0041:     elif isinstance(filters, dict) and filters:
0042:         encoded = urlencode(
0043:             {k: v for k, v in filters.items() if v is not None},
0044:             doseq=True,
0045:         )
0046:         if encoded:
0047:             url = f"{url}?{encoded}"
0048:     return url
0049: 
0050: 
0051: def _parse_json_list(res: httpx.Response, operation: str, table: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
0052:     if not res.text or not res.text.strip():
0053:         return []
0054:     try:
0055:         parsed = res.json()
0056:     except ValueError as exc:
0057:         print(f"[{operation} JSON ERROR] table={table} error={exc} body={res.text}")
0058:         raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail=f"Database {operation.lower()} operation failed")
0059:     if isinstance(parsed, list):
0060:         return parsed
0061:     if isinstance(parsed, dict):
0062:         return [parsed]
0063:     return []
0064: 
0065: 
0066: async def sb_select(
0067:     table: str,
0068:     params: dict[str, Any] | str | None = None,
0069: ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
0070:     url = _build_url(table, params)
0071:     try:
0072:         async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=15.0) as client:
0073:             res = await client.get(url, headers=_headers())
0074:     except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
0075:         print(f"[DB READ NETWORK ERROR] table={table} url={url} error={exc}")
0076:         raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="Database read operation failed")
0077: 
0078:     if res.status_code >= 400:
0079:         print(f"[DB READ ERROR] table={table} status={res.status_code} url={url} body={res.text}")
0080:         raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="Database read operation failed")
0081: 
0082:     return _parse_json_list(res, "READ", table)
0083: 
0084: 
0085: async def sb_insert(
0086:     table: str,
0087:     payload: dict[str, Any] | list[dict[str, Any]],
0088: ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
0089:     url = _build_url(table)
0090:     try:
0091:         async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=15.0) as client:
0092:             res = await client.post(url, headers=_headers(), json=payload)
0093:     except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
0094:         print(f"[DB INSERT NETWORK ERROR] table={table} url={url} error={exc}")
0095:         raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="Database write operation failed")
0096: 
0097:     if res.status_code >= 400:
0098:         print(f"[DB INSERT ERROR] table={table} status={res.status_code} url={url} body={res.text}")
0099:         raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="Database write operation failed")
0100: 
0101:     return _parse_json_list(res, "INSERT", table)
0102: 
0103: 
0104: async def sb_update(
0105:     table: str,
0106:     payload: dict[str, Any],
0107:     params: dict[str, Any] | str | None = None,
0108: ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
0109:     url = _build_url(table, params)
0110:     try:
0111:         async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=15.0) as client:
0112:             res = await client.patch(url, headers=_headers(), json=payload)
0113:     except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
0114:         print(f"[DB UPDATE NETWORK ERROR] table={table} url={url} error={exc}")
0115:         raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="Database update operation failed")
0116: 
0117:     if res.status_code >= 400:
0118:         print(f"[DB UPDATE ERROR] table={table} status={res.status_code} url={url} body={res.text}")
0119:         raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="Database update operation failed")
0120: 
0121:     return _parse_json_list(res, "UPDATE", table)
0122: 
0123: 
0124: async def sb_delete(
0125:     table: str,
0126:     params: dict[str, Any] | str | None = None,
0127: ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
0128:     url = _build_url(table, params)
0129:     try:
0130:         async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=15.0) as client:
0131:             res = await client.delete(url, headers=_headers())
0132:     except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
0133:         print(f"[DB DELETE NETWORK ERROR] table={table} url={url} error={exc}")
0134:         raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="Database delete operation failed")
0135: 
0136:     if res.status_code >= 400:
0137:         print(f"[DB DELETE ERROR] table={table} status={res.status_code} url={url} body={res.text}")
0138:         raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="Database delete operation failed")
0139: 
0140:     return _parse_json_list(res, "DELETE", table)

0157:

0159:     _set_refresh_cookie(response, raw_refresh)
0160:     await log_audit(request, "register", user["id"], email, "user", user["id"], "New user registered")
0161:     return {"access_token": access_token, "token_type": "bearer", "user": _public_user(user)}

0164: @app.post("/api/auth/login")
0165: async def login(payload: LoginRequest, request: Request, response: Response):
0166:     email = payload.email.strip().lower()
0167:     users = await sb_select("users", f"email=eq.{quote(email)}&select=*")
0168:     if not users:
0169:         await log_audit(request, "failed_login", None, email, "auth", email, "Unknown email")
0170:         raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Invalid email or password")
0171: 
0172:     user = users[0]
0173:     now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
0174: 
0175:     if user.get("locked_until"):
0176:         locked_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(str(user["locked_until"]).replace("Z", "+00:00"))
0177:         if locked_dt > now:
0178:             await log_audit(request, "failed_login", user["id"], email, "auth", user["id"], "Account locked")
0179:             raise HTTPException(
0180:                 status_code=status.HTTP_429_TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,
0181:                 detail="Too many failed login attempts. Try again in 15 minutes.",
0182:             )
0183: 
0184:     if user.get("is_banned"):
0185:         await log_audit(request, "failed_login", user["id"], email, "auth", user["id"], "Banned user login attempt")
0186:         raise HTTPException(
0187:             status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
0188:             detail=f"Account banned: {user.get('ban_reason') or 'Contact administrator'}",
0189:         )
0190: 
0191:     if not verify_password(payload.password, user["password_hash"]):
0192:         attempts = int(user.get("failed_login_attempts") or 0) + 1
0193:         update_data: dict = {"failed_login_attempts": attempts}
0194:         if attempts >= MAX_FAILED_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS:
0195:             update_data["locked_until"] = (now + timedelta(minutes=LOCKOUT_MINUTES)).isoformat()
0196:         await sb_update("users", update_data, f"id=eq.{user['id']}")
0197:         await log_audit(
0198:             request,
0199:             "failed_login",
0200:             user["id"],
0201:             email,
0202:             "auth",
0203:             user["id"],
0204:             f"Wrong password (attempt {attempts})",
0205:         )
0206:         raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Invalid email or password")
0207: 
0208:     await sb_update("users", {"failed_login_attempts": 0, "locked_until": None}, f"id=eq.{user['id']}")
0209: 
0210:     raw_refresh = generate_refresh_token()
0211:     ip, ua = get_client_meta(request)
0212:     session = (
0213:         await sb_insert(
0214:             "sessions",
0215:             {
0216:                 "user_id": user["id"],
0217:                 "refresh_token_hash": hash_refresh_token(raw_refresh),
0218:                 "ip_address": ip,
0219:                 "user_agent": ua,
0220:                 "expires_at": (now + timedelta(days=REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS)).isoformat(),
0221:             },
0222:         )
0223:     )[0]
0224: 
0225:     access_token = create_access_token(user["id"], user["role"], session["id"])
0226:     _set_refresh_cookie(response, raw_refresh)
0227:     await log_audit(request, "login", user["id"], email, "session", session["id"], "Successful login")
0228:     return {"access_token": access_token, "token_type": "bearer", "user": _public_user(user)}

0231: @app.post("/api/auth/refresh")
0232: async def refresh_session(request: Request, response: Response):
0233:     raw_refresh = request.cookies.get("df_refresh_token")
0234:     if not raw_refresh:
0235:         raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Missing refresh cookie")
0236: 
0237:     token_hash = hash_refresh_token(raw_refresh)
0238:     sessions = await sb_select("sessions", f"refresh_token_hash=eq.{token_hash}&select=*")
0239:     if not sessions:
0240:         _clear_refresh_cookie(response)
0241:         raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Invalid refresh token")
0242: 
0243:     session = sessions[0]
0244:     now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
0245:     expires_at = datetime.fromisoformat(str(session["expires_at"]).replace("Z", "+00:00"))
0246:     if session.get("revoked") or expires_at <= now:
0247:         _clear_refresh_cookie(response)
0248:         raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Refresh session expired or revoked")
0249: 
0250:     users = await sb_select("users", f"id=eq.{session['user_id']}&select=*")
0251:     if not users or users[0].get("is_banned"):
0252:         await sb_update("sessions", {"revoked": True}, f"id=eq.{session['id']}")
0253:         _clear_refresh_cookie(response)
0254:         raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Account unavailable or banned")
0255: 
0256:     user = users[0]
0257:     new_raw_refresh = generate_refresh_token()
0258:     await sb_update(
0259:         "sessions",
0260:         {"refresh_token_hash": hash_refresh_token(new_raw_refresh)},
0261:         f"id=eq.{session['id']}",
0262:     )
0263:     access_token = create_access_token(user["id"], user["role"], session["id"])
0264:     _set_refresh_cookie(response, new_raw_refresh)
0265:     return {"access_token": access_token, "token_type": "bearer", "user": _public_user(user)}
0266: 
0267: 
0268: @app.post("/api/auth/logout")
0269: async def logout(request: Request, response: Response):
0270:     raw_refresh = request.cookies.get("df_refresh_token")
0271:     if raw_refresh:
0272:         token_hash = hash_refresh_token(raw_refresh)
0273:         sessions = await sb_select("sessions", f"refresh_token_hash=eq.{token_hash}&select=*")
0274:         if sessions:
0275:             sess = sessions[0]
0276:             await sb_update("sessions", {"revoked": True}, f"id=eq.{sess['id']}")
0277:             users = await sb_select("users", f"id=eq.{sess['user_id']}&select=email")
0278:             email = users[0]["email"] if users else "unknown"
0279:             await log_audit(request, "logout", sess["user_id"], email, "session", sess["id"], "User logged out")
0280:     _clear_refresh_cookie(response)
0281:     return {"logged_out": True}
0282:

0381: 
0382: # ==========================================
0383: # 2. GROUPS / TEAMS WITH FINE-GRAINED PERMISSIONS
0384: # ==========================================
0385: @app.get("/api/groups")
0386: async def list_groups(current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user)):
0387:     all_groups = await sb_select("groups", "order=created_at.desc&select=*")
0388:     all_members = await sb_select("group_members", "select=*")
0389:     users = await sb_select("users", "select=id,email,full_name")
0390:     user_map = {u["id"]: u for u in users}
0391: 
0392:     result = []
0393:     for g in all_groups:
0394:         g_members = [m for m in all_members if m["group_id"] == g["id"]]
0395:         my_mem = next((m for m in g_members if m["user_id"] == current_user["id"]), None)
0396:         is_owner = g["owner_id"] == current_user["id"]
0397: 
0398:         if current_user["role"] != ROLE_ADMIN and not is_owner and not my_mem:
0399:             continue
0400: 
0401:         enriched_members = []
0402:         for m in g_members:
0403:             u_info = user_map.get(m["user_id"], {})
0404:             enriched_members.append(
0405:                 {
0406:                     **m,
0407:                     "email": u_info.get("email", "unknown"),
0408:                     "full_name": u_info.get("full_name", "Unknown User"),
0409:                 }
0410:             )
0411: 
0412:         result.append(
0413:             {
0414:                 **g,
0415:                 "owner_name": user_map.get(g["owner_id"], {}).get("full_name", "Owner"),
0416:                 "is_owner": is_owner,
0417:                 "my_permissions": {
0418:                     "can_create": True if (is_owner or current_user["role"] == ROLE_ADMIN) else bool(my_mem and my_mem["can_create"]),
0419:                     "can_edit": True if (is_owner or current_user["role"] == ROLE_ADMIN) else bool(my_mem and my_mem["can_edit"]),
0420:                     "can_assign": True if (is_owner or current_user["role"] == ROLE_ADMIN) else bool(my_mem and my_mem["can_assign"]),
0421:                     "can_manage": is_owner or current_user["role"] == ROLE_ADMIN,
0422:                 },
0423:                 "members": enriched_members,
0424:             }
0425:         )
0426:     return result
0427:

0512: @app.post("/api/groups/{group_id}/members")
0513: async def upsert_group_member(
0514:     group_id: str,
0515:     payload: GroupMemberUpsert,
0516:     request: Request,
0517:     current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user),
0518: ):
0519:     group, _ = await verify_group_permission(group_id, current_user, owner_only=True)
0520:     email = payload.user_email.strip().lower()
0521:     users = await sb_select("users", f"email=eq.{quote(email)}&select=*")
0522:     if not users:
0523:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Target user email not found")
0524:     target_user = users[0]
0525: 
0526:     existing = await sb_select(
0527:         "group_members",
0528:         f"group_id=eq.{group_id}&user_id=eq.{target_user['id']}&select=*",
0529:     )
0530:     is_owner = target_user["id"] == group["owner_id"]
0531:     mem_data = {
0532:         "member_role": "owner" if is_owner else "member",
0533:         "can_create": True if is_owner else payload.can_create,
0534:         "can_edit": True if is_owner else payload.can_edit,
0535:         "can_assign": True if is_owner else payload.can_assign,
0536:     }
0537:     if existing:
0538:         saved = (
0539:             await sb_update(
0540:                 "group_members",
0541:                 mem_data,
0542:                 f"group_id=eq.{group_id}&user_id=eq.{target_user['id']}",
0543:             )
0544:         )[0]
0545:     else:
0546:         saved = (
0547:             await sb_insert(
0548:                 "group_members",
0549:                 {"group_id": group_id, "user_id": target_user["id"], **mem_data},
0550:             )
0551:         )[0]
0552: 
0553:     await log_audit(
0554:         request,
0555:         "group_member_update",
0556:         current_user["id"],
0557:         current_user["email"],
0558:         "group",
0559:         group_id,
0560:         f"Updated member {email} permissions (create={mem_data['can_create']}, edit={mem_data['can_edit']}, assign={mem_data['can_assign']})",
0561:     )
0562:     return saved
0563:

0588: # ==========================================
0589: # 3. CROP LISTINGS (OWNERSHIP + GROUP RBAC)
0590: # ==========================================
0591: async def _accessible_group_ids(user_id: str) -> set[str]:
0592:     mems = await sb_select("group_members", f"user_id=eq.{user_id}&select=group_id")
0593:     owned = await sb_select("groups", f"owner_id=eq.{user_id}&select=id")
0594:     return {m["group_id"] for m in mems} | {g["id"] for g in owned}
0595: 
0596: 
0597: @app.get("/api/crops")
0598: async def list_crops(current_user: dict = Depends(require_permission(PERM_CROPS_READ))):
0599:     all_crops = await sb_select("crop_listings", "order=id.desc&select=*")
0600:     if current_user["role"] == ROLE_ADMIN:
0601:         return all_crops
0602: 
0603:     group_ids = await _accessible_group_ids(current_user["id"])
0604:     return [
0605:         c
0606:         for c in all_crops
0607:         if c["owner_id"] == current_user["id"] or (c.get("group_id") and c["group_id"] in group_ids)
0608:     ]
0609: 
0610: 
0611: @app.get("/api/crops/{crop_id}")
0612: async def get_crop(crop_id: int, current_user: dict = Depends(require_permission(PERM_CROPS_READ))):
0613:     rows = await sb_select("crop_listings", f"id=eq.{crop_id}&select=*")
0614:     if not rows:
0615:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Crop listing not found")
0616:     crop = rows[0]
0617: 
0618:     if current_user["role"] != ROLE_ADMIN:
0619:         group_ids = await _accessible_group_ids(current_user["id"])
0620:         if crop["owner_id"] != current_user["id"] and crop.get("group_id") not in group_ids:
0621:             raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Access denied to another user's crop listing")
0622:     return crop
0623: 
0624:

0626: async def create_crop(

0632:         await verify_group_permission(payload.group_id, current_user, required_perm="create")

0637:     data["created_at"] = now
0638:     data["updated_at"] = now

0653: @app.put("/api/crops/{crop_id}")
0654: async def update_crop(
0655:     crop_id: int,
0656:     payload: CropUpdate,
0657:     request: Request,
0658:     current_user: dict = Depends(require_permission(PERM_CROPS_EDIT)),
0659: ):
0660:     rows = await sb_select("crop_listings", f"id=eq.{crop_id}&select=*")
0661:     if not rows:
0662:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Crop listing not found")
0663:     crop = rows[0]
0664: 
0665:     updates = payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
0666:     if not updates:
0667:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No fields provided")
0668: 
0669:     if current_user["role"] != ROLE_ADMIN:
0670:         if crop.get("group_id"):
0671:             only_assign = set(updates.keys()) == {"assigned_to"}
0672:             if only_assign:
0673:                 await verify_group_permission(crop["group_id"], current_user, required_perm="assign")
0674:             else:
0675:                 await verify_group_permission(crop["group_id"], current_user, required_perm="edit")
0676:                 if "assigned_to" in updates and updates["assigned_to"] != crop.get("assigned_to"):
0677:                     await verify_group_permission(crop["group_id"], current_user, required_perm="assign")
0678:         elif crop["owner_id"] != current_user["id"]:
0679:             raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="You can only update your own crop listings")
0680: 
0681:     updates["updated_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
0682:     updated = (await sb_update("crop_listings", updates, f"id=eq.{crop_id}"))[0]
0683:     await log_audit(
0684:         request,
0685:         "crop_update",
0686:         current_user["id"],
0687:         current_user["email"],
0688:         "crop",
0689:         str(crop_id),
0690:         f"Updated crop '{updated['crop_name']}'",
0691:     )
0692:     return updated
0693: 
0694: 
0695: @app.delete("/api/crops/{crop_id}")
0696: async def delete_crop(
0697:     crop_id: int,
0698:     request: Request,
0699:     current_user: dict = Depends(require_permission(PERM_CROPS_DELETE)),
0700: ):

0702:     if not rows:
0703:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Crop listing not found")
0704:     crop = rows[0]
0705: 
0706:     if current_user["role"] != ROLE_ADMIN:
0707:         if crop.get("group_id"):
0708:             await verify_group_permission(crop["group_id"], current_user, owner_only=True)
0709:         elif crop["owner_id"] != current_user["id"]:
0710:             raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="You can only delete your own crop listings")
0711: 
0712:     await sb_delete("crop_listings", f"id=eq.{crop_id}")
0713:     await log_audit(
0714:         request,
0715:         "delete",
0716:         current_user["id"],
0717:         current_user["email"],
0718:         "crop",
0719:         str(crop_id),

0724: 
0725: # ==========================================
0726: # 4. ADMIN DASHBOARD, MODERATION, AUDIT & CSV EXPORTS
0727: # ==========================================
0728: @app.get("/api/admin/overview")
0729: async def admin_overview(admin: dict = Depends(require_role(ROLE_ADMIN))):
0730:     users = await sb_select("users", "select=id,is_banned,created_at")
0731:     crops = await sb_select("crop_listings", "select=id,created_at")
0732:     groups = await sb_select("groups", "select=id,created_at")
0733: 
0734:     cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=7)
0735: 
0736:     def is_recent(ts: str | None) -> bool:
0737:         if not ts:
0738:             return False
0739:         return datetime.fromisoformat(str(ts).replace("Z", "+00:00")) >= cutoff
0740: 
0741:     return {
0742:         "total_users": len(users),
0743:         "banned_users": sum(1 for u in users if u.get("is_banned")),
0744:         "total_records": len(crops),
0745:         "total_groups": len(groups),
0746:         "new_users_7d": sum(1 for u in users if is_recent(u.get("created_at"))),
0747:         "new_records_7d": sum(1 for c in crops if is_recent(c.get("created_at"))),
0748:         "new_groups_7d": sum(1 for g in groups if is_recent(g.get("created_at"))),
0749:     }
0750: 
0751: 
0752: def _filter_users(users: list[dict], search: str, role: str, status_filter: str) -> list[dict]:
0753:     out = users
0754:     if search:
0755:         q = search.lower()
0756:         out = [u for u in out if q in u["email"].lower() or q in u["full_name"].lower() or q in u.get("district", "").lower()]
0757:     if role and role != "all":
0758:         out = [u for u in out if u["role"] == role]
0759:     if status_filter == "banned":
0760:         out = [u for u in out if u.get("is_banned")]
0761:     elif status_filter == "active":
0762:         out = [u for u in out if not u.get("is_banned")]
0763:     return out
0764: 
0765: 
0766: @app.get("/api/admin/users")
0767: async def admin_list_users(
0768:     search: str = "",
0769:     role: str = "all",
0770:     status_filter: str = Query(default="all", alias="status"),
0771:     page: int = Query(default=1, ge=1),
0772:     page_size: int = Query(default=10, ge=1, le=100),
0773:     admin: dict = Depends(require_role(ROLE_ADMIN)),
0774: ):
0775:     users = await sb_select("users", "order=created_at.desc&select=*")
0776:     filtered = [_public_user(u) for u in _filter_users(users, search, role, status_filter)]
0777:     total = len(filtered)
0778:     start = (page - 1) * page_size
0779:     return {"items": filtered[start : start + page_size], "total": total, "page": page, "page_size": page_size}

0891: @app.post("/api/admin/users/bulk-ban")
0892: async def admin_bulk_ban(
0893:     payload: BulkBanRequest,
0894:     request: Request,
0895:     admin: dict = Depends(require_role(ROLE_ADMIN)),
0896: ):
0897:     affected = 0
0898:     for uid in payload.user_ids:
0899:         users = await sb_select("users", f"id=eq.{uid}&select=*")
0900:         if not users:
0901:             continue
0902:         target = users[0]
0903:         if target["id"] == admin["id"] or target.get("role") == ROLE_ADMIN:
0904:             continue
0905:         await sb_update(
0906:             "users",
0907:             {"is_banned": payload.ban, "ban_reason": payload.reason.strip() if payload.ban else None},
0908:             f"id=eq.{uid}",
0909:         )
0910:         if payload.ban:
0911:             await sb_update("sessions", {"revoked": True}, f"user_id=eq.{uid}")
0912:         affected += 1
0913: 
0914:     await log_audit(
0915:         request,
0916:         "ban" if payload.ban else "unban",
0917:         admin["id"],
0918:         admin["email"],
0919:         "bulk_users",
0920:         ",".join(payload.user_ids),
0921:         f"Bulk {'ban' if payload.ban else 'unban'} applied to {affected} users",
0922:     )
0923:     return {"affected": affected}

0989:         out = [
0990:             l

0992:             if q in l.get("user_email", "").lower()
0993:             or q in l.get("ip_address", "").lower()
0994:             or q in l.get("details", "").lower()
0995:             or q in l.get("action", "").lower()
0996:         ]

1010: @app.get("/api/admin/security")
1011: async def admin_security_summary(admin: dict = Depends(require_role(ROLE_ADMIN))):
1012:     failed = await sb_select("audit_logs", "action=eq.failed_login&order=id.desc&limit=200&select=*")
1013:     by_ip: dict[str, int] = {}
1014:     by_account: dict[str, int] = {}
1015:     for entry in failed:
1016:         ip = entry.get("ip_address") or "unknown"
1017:         acct = entry.get("user_email") or "unknown"
1018:         by_ip[ip] = by_ip.get(ip, 0) + 1
1019:         by_account[acct] = by_account.get(acct, 0) + 1
1020:     return {
1021:         "by_ip": [{"ip_address": k, "count": v} for k, v in sorted(by_ip.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])],
1022:         "by_account": [{"email": k, "count": v} for k, v in sorted(by_account.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])],
1023:         "recent_failures": failed[:20],
1024:     }

1029:     writer = csv.writer(buf)

1031:     writer.writerows(rows)
1032:     buf.seek(0)
1033:     return StreamingResponse(
1034:         iter([buf.getvalue()]),
1035:         media_type="text/csv",
1036:         headers={"Content-Disposition": f'attachment; filename="{filename}"'},

0001: from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
0002: from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials, HTTPBearer
0003: from db import sb_select
0004: from security import decode_access_token
0005: 
0006: bearer_scheme = HTTPBearer(auto_error=False)
0007: 
0008: # Centralized Role & Permission Constants
0009: ROLE_USER = "user"
0010: ROLE_ADMIN = "admin"
0011: 
0012: PERM_ADMIN_ALL = "admin:all"
0013: PERM_CROPS_READ = "crops:read"
0014: PERM_CROPS_CREATE = "crops:create"
0015: PERM_CROPS_EDIT = "crops:edit"
0016: PERM_CROPS_DELETE = "crops:delete"
0017: 
0018: GROUP_PERM_CREATE = "create"
0019: GROUP_PERM_EDIT = "edit"
0020: GROUP_PERM_ASSIGN = "assign"
0021: 
0022: ROLE_PERMISSIONS: dict[str, set[str]] = {
0023:     ROLE_USER: {PERM_CROPS_READ, PERM_CROPS_CREATE, PERM_CROPS_EDIT, PERM_CROPS_DELETE},
0024:     ROLE_ADMIN: {
0025:         PERM_ADMIN_ALL,
0026:         PERM_CROPS_READ,
0027:         PERM_CROPS_CREATE,
0028:         PERM_CROPS_EDIT,
0029:         PERM_CROPS_DELETE,
0030:     },
0031: }

0033: 
0034: async def get_current_user(
0035:     creds: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials | None = Depends(bearer_scheme),
0036: ) -> dict:
0037:     if not creds or creds.scheme.lower() != "bearer":
0038:         raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Missing bearer token")
0039: 
0040:     payload = decode_access_token(creds.credentials)
0041:     user_id = payload["sub"]
0042:     session_id = payload.get("sid")
0043: 
0044:     if session_id:
0045:         sessions = await sb_select("sessions", f"id=eq.{session_id}&select=id,revoked")
0046:         if not sessions or sessions[0].get("revoked"):
0047:             raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Session has been revoked")
0048: 
0049:     users = await sb_select("users", f"id=eq.{user_id}&select=*")
0050:     if not users:
0051:         raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="User not found")
0052: 
0053:     user = users[0]
0054:     if user.get("is_banned"):
0055:         raise HTTPException(
0056:             status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
0057:             detail=f"Account banned: {user.get('ban_reason') or 'Policy violation'}",
0058:         )
0059:     user["session_id"] = session_id
0060:     return user
0061: 
0062: 
0063: def require_role(*allowed_roles: str):
0064:     async def dependency(current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user)) -> dict:
0065:         if current_user.get("role") not in allowed_roles:
0066:             raise HTTPException(
0067:                 status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
0068:                 detail=f"Requires role: {', '.join(allowed_roles)}",
0069:             )
0070:         return current_user
0071: 
0072:     return dependency
0073: 
0074: 
0075: def require_permission(permission: str):
0076:     async def dependency(current_user: dict = Depends(get_current_user)) -> dict:
0077:         perms = ROLE_PERMISSIONS.get(current_user.get("role", ""), set())
0078:         if permission not in perms and PERM_ADMIN_ALL not in perms:
0079:             raise HTTPException(
0080:                 status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
0081:                 detail=f"Missing required permission: {permission}",
0082:             )
0083:         return current_user
0084: 
0085:     return dependency
0086: 
0087: 
0088: async def get_group_membership(group_id: str, user_id: str) -> dict | None:
0089:     rows = await sb_select(
0090:         "group_members",
0091:         f"group_id=eq.{group_id}&user_id=eq.{user_id}&select=*",
0092:     )
0093:     return rows[0] if rows else None
0094: 
0095: 
0096: async def verify_group_permission(
0097:     group_id: str,
0098:     current_user: dict,
0099:     required_perm: str | None = None,
0100:     owner_only: bool = False,
0101: ) -> tuple[dict, dict | None]:
0102:     groups = await sb_select("groups", f"id=eq.{group_id}&select=*")
0103:     if not groups:
0104:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Group not found")
0105:     group = groups[0]
0106: 
0107:     if current_user.get("role") == ROLE_ADMIN:
0108:         return group, None
0109: 
0110:     is_owner = group["owner_id"] == current_user["id"]
0111:     membership = await get_group_membership(group_id, current_user["id"])
0112: 
0113:     if owner_only and not is_owner:
0114:         raise HTTPException(
0115:             status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
0116:             detail="Only the group owner can delete, rename, or manage members",
0117:         )
0118: 
0119:     if not is_owner and not membership:
0120:         raise HTTPException(
0121:             status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
0122:             detail="You are not a member of this group",
0123:         )
0124: 
0125:     if required_perm and not is_owner:
0126:         perm_col = f"can_{required_perm}"
0127:         if not membership or not membership.get(perm_col):
0128:             raise HTTPException(
0129:                 status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
0130:                 detail=f"Group permission '{required_perm}' denied for your membership",
0131:             )
0132: 
0133:     return group, membership
0134: 
0135: 
0136: def enforce_admin_target_safety(actor: dict, target: dict, action_label: str) -> None:
0137:     if actor["id"] == target["id"]:
0138:         raise HTTPException(
0139:             status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
0140:             detail=f"Admins cannot {action_label} their own account",
0141:         )
0142:     if target.get("role") == ROLE_ADMIN:
0143:         raise HTTPException(
0144:             status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
0145:             detail=f"Admins cannot {action_label} another admin account",
0146:         )

=== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\DigitalFarmers_2331515\backend\security.py ===
0001: from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
0002: import hashlib
0003: import hmac
0004: import secrets
0005: from argon2 import PasswordHasher
0006: from argon2.exceptions import VerifyMismatchError
0007: from fastapi import HTTPException, Request, status
0008: import jwt
0009: from config import (
0010:     ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES,
0011:     JWT_ALGORITHM,
0012:     JWT_SECRET_KEY,
0013:     PASSWORD_PEPPER,
0014: )
0015: from db import sb_insert
0016: 
0017: _ph = PasswordHasher()
0018: 
0019: 
0020: def _apply_pepper(plain_password: str) -> str:
0021:     return hmac.new(
0022:         PASSWORD_PEPPER.encode("utf-8"),
0023:         plain_password.encode("utf-8"),
0024:         hashlib.sha256,
0025:     ).hexdigest()
0026: 
0027: 
0028: def hash_password(plain_password: str) -> str:
0029:     return _ph.hash(_apply_pepper(plain_password))
0030: 
0031: 
0032: def verify_password(plain_password: str, password_hash: str) -> bool:
0033:     try:
0034:         return _ph.verify(password_hash, _apply_pepper(plain_password))
0035:     except VerifyMismatchError:
0036:         return False
0037:     except Exception:
0038:         return False
0039: 
0040: 
0041: def create_access_token(user_id: str, role: str, session_id: str) -> str:
0042:     now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
0043:     payload = {
0044:         "sub": user_id,
0045:         "role": role,
0046:         "sid": session_id,
0047:         "type": "access",
0048:         "iat": int(now.timestamp()),
0049:         "exp": int((now + timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES)).timestamp()),
0050:     }
0051:     return jwt.encode(payload, JWT_SECRET_KEY, algorithm=JWT_ALGORITHM)
0052: 
0053: 
0054: def decode_access_token(token: str) -> dict:
0055:     try:
0056:         payload = jwt.decode(token, JWT_SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[JWT_ALGORITHM])
0057:         if payload.get("type") != "access" or not payload.get("sub"):
0058:             raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Invalid token payload")
0059:         return payload
0060:     except jwt.ExpiredSignatureError:
0061:         raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Access token expired")
0062:     except jwt.InvalidTokenError:
0063:         raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Invalid or tampered token")
0064: 
0065: 
0066: def generate_refresh_token() -> str:
0067:     return secrets.token_urlsafe(48)
0068: 
0069: 
0070: def hash_refresh_token(raw_token: str) -> str:
0071:     return hashlib.sha256(f"{PASSWORD_PEPPER}:{raw_token}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
0072: 
0073: 
0074: def get_client_meta(request: Request) -> tuple[str, str]:
0075:     forwarded = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for", "")
0076:     ip = forwarded.split(",")[0].strip() if forwarded else (request.client.host if request.client else "unknown")
0077:     ua = request.headers.get("user-agent", "unknown")[:250]
0078:     return ip, ua
0079: 
0080: 
0081: async def log_audit(
0082:     request: Request,
0083:     action: str,
0084:     user_id: str | None = None,
0085:     user_email: str = "anonymous",
0086:     target_type: str = "system",
0087:     target_id: str = "",
0088:     details: str = "",
0089: ) -> None:
0090:     ip, ua = get_client_meta(request)
0091:     try:
0092:         await sb_insert(
0093:             "audit_logs",
0094:             {
0095:                 "user_id": user_id,
0096:                 "user_email": user_email,
0097:                 "action": action,
0098:                 "target_type": target_type,
0099:                 "target_id": str(target_id),
0100:                 "details": details,
0101:                 "ip_address": ip,
0102:                 "user_agent": ua,
0103:             },
0104:         )
0105:     except Exception:
0106:         pass

=== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\DigitalFarmers_2331515\docs\PRD.md ===
0001: # Product Requirements Document (PRD)
0002: **Project Title:** DigitalFarmers — Direct Farmer-to-Buyer Crop Marketplace  
0003: **Repository:** `https://github.com/mahamud-tech/DigitalFarmers_2331515`  
0004: **Student ID:** 2331515  
0005: **Tech Stack:** React (TypeScript), FastAPI (Python), Supabase (PostgreSQL), Vercel  
0006: 
0007: ---
0008: 
0009: ## 1. Problem Statement & Vision
0010: Smallholder farmers in Bangladesh frequently rely on layered middlemen to sell harvested produce, reducing farm-gate profit margins while obscuring real-time supply and pricing for local buyers. **DigitalFarmers** provides a clean, lightweight digital marketplace where farmers can publish available crop harvests and buyers can browse transparent quantities, unit prices, and district locations directly.
0011: 
0012: ---
0013: 
0014: ## 2. Target Users & Personas
0015: | Persona | Role | Primary Goal | Release |
0016: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
0017: | **Farmer** | Producer / Seller | List harvested crops with quantity (kg), price per kg (BDT), and district; update stock or mark harvests as sold. | **MVP** & **Beta** |
0018: | **Buyer** | Wholesale / Retail Purchaser | Browse available produce listings, inspect single crop details, and place purchase inquiries/orders. | **MVP** & **Beta** |
0019: | **Admin** | Platform Moderator | Verify farmer accounts, audit crop listings, and manage role permissions. | **Beta** |
0020: 
0021: ---
0022: 
0023: ## 3. Release Roadmap (MVP vs. Beta)
0024: 
0025: ### Release 1: Minimum Viable Product (MVP — Mid-Term Demo)
0026: Focuses on end-to-end core domain functionality running locally via REST APIs and persisted in Supabase:
0027: - **[MVP]** **Create Crop Listing:** Add a new crop entry (`farmer_name`, `crop_name`, `category`, `quantity_kg`, `price_per_kg`, `location`, `status`).
0028: - **[MVP]** **Read Crop Listings (List View):** Fetch and display all crop listings in a structured interface.
0029: - **[MVP]** **Read Single Crop Listing (Detail View):** Inspect full metadata and timestamps of a single crop listing by ID.
0030: - **[MVP]** **Update Crop Listing:** Edit existing crop price, available quantity, location, or availability status (`available`, `reserved`, `sold`).
0031: - **[MVP]** **Delete Crop Listing:** Remove obsolete or erroneous crop entries from the marketplace.
0032: 
0033: ### Release 2: Beta Release (Final Demo)
0034: Extends the MVP with security, multi-user ownership, personalization, and cloud deployment:
0035: - **[Beta]** **User Authentication:** Sign up, login, and JWT/session token verification via Supabase Auth / FastAPI dependencies.
0036: - **[Beta]** **Role-Based Access Control (RBAC):** Distinct roles (`farmer`, `buyer`, `admin`) with enforced API security scopes (`crops:write`, `crops:read`, `admin:manage`).
0037: - **[Beta]** **User Profile Dashboard:** Personalized dashboard showing a farmer's own active/sold listings or a buyer's saved/ordered crops.
0038: - **[Beta]** **Production Cloud Deployment:** Full-stack deployment on Vercel with environment-configured Supabase PostgreSQL persistence.
0039: 
0040: ---
0041: 
0042: ## 4. User Stories & Acceptance Criteria
0043: 
0044: | ID | User Story | Acceptance Criteria | Release |
0045: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
0046: | **US-01** | As a farmer, I want to publish a crop listing so buyers can see my available harvest. | Submitting valid crop fields creates a record (`201 Created`) and displays it in the listing feed. | **MVP** |
0047: | **US-02** | As a buyer, I want to view all available crop listings so I can compare prices and quantities. | Opening the app loads all crop records (`200 OK`) with crop name, farmer, quantity, price, and status. | **MVP** |
0048: | **US-03** | As a buyer, I want to inspect a single crop listing so I can verify complete harvest details. | Selecting **View** fetches `/api/crops/{id}` (`200 OK`) and displays full details and timestamps. | **MVP** |
0049: | **US-04** | As a farmer, I want to update my crop's price, quantity, or status when stock changes. | Editing a listing sends `PUT /api/crops/{id}` (`200 OK`) and immediately updates the UI. | **MVP** |
0050: | **US-05** | As a farmer, I want to delete a listing posted by mistake. | Clicking **Delete** sends `DELETE /api/crops/{id}` (`200 OK`) and removes the card from the UI. | **MVP** |
0051: | **US-06** | As a registered user, I want to log in securely so only I can modify or delete my own listings. | Unauthenticated or unauthorized `PUT`/`DELETE` requests return `401`/`403` based on RBAC scopes. | **Beta** |
0052: | **US-07** | As a farmer or buyer, I want a role-specific profile dashboard to manage my listings and orders. | Authenticated users see filtered metrics and listings tied to their `user_id`. | **Beta** |
0053: 
0054: ---
0055: 
0056: ## 5. Out of Scope (To Keep Scope Realistic)
0057: - Automated mobile banking / payment gateway processing (bKash/Nagad integration).
0058: - Real-time GPS fleet tracking for agricultural transport trucks.
0059: - AI crop disease image diagnosis.

=== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\DigitalFarmers_2331515\docs\SRS.md ===
0001: # Software Requirements Specification (SRS)
0002: **Project Title:** DigitalFarmers  
0003: **Version:** 1.0  
0004: **Target Releases:** MVP (Mid-Term) & Beta (Final)  
0005: 
0006: ---
0007: 
0008: ## 1. System Overview
0009: DigitalFarmers is a decoupled client-server web application built with a **React + TypeScript** single-page frontend and a **FastAPI (Python)** RESTful backend connected to **Supabase (PostgreSQL)**. In local development, Vite proxies `/api/*` requests to FastAPI (`localhost:8000`); in production, Vercel routes `/api/*` to the FastAPI serverless entrypoint and `/*` to the static Vite bundle.
0010: 
0011: ---
0012: 
0013: ## 2. Functional Requirements
0014: 
0015: ### 2.1 Crop Listings Management (Core Domain)
0016: | Req ID | Requirement Description | Release |
0017: | :--- | :--- | :--- |
0018: | **FR-01** | The system shall allow creating a new crop listing via `POST /api/crops` with validated `farmer_name`, `crop_name`, `category`, `quantity_kg > 0`, `price_per_kg > 0`, `location`, and `status`. | **MVP** |
0019: | **FR-02** | The system shall retrieve and display a list of all crop listings via `GET /api/crops`. | **MVP** |
0020: | **FR-03** | The system shall retrieve and display a single crop listing by integer `id` via `GET /api/crops/{crop_id}`, returning HTTP `404` if not found. | **MVP** |
0021: | **FR-04** | The system shall allow updating one or more fields of an existing crop listing via `PUT /api/crops/{crop_id}`, updating `updated_at` and returning HTTP `404` if not found. | **MVP** |
0022: | **FR-05** | The system shall allow deleting an existing crop listing via `DELETE /api/crops/{crop_id}`, returning HTTP `404` if not found. | **MVP** |
0023: | **FR-06** | The frontend shall provide interactive forms, single-item detail inspection, inline edit state, and delete actions without full page reloads. | **MVP** |
0024: 
0025: ### 2.2 Authentication, RBAC & User Dashboard
0026: | Req ID | Requirement Description | Release |
0027: | :--- | :--- | :--- |
0028: | **FR-07** | The system shall provide user registration and login endpoints issuing signed Bearer JWT tokens. | **Beta** |
0029: | **FR-08** | The system shall enforce Role-Based Access Control (`farmer`, `buyer`, `admin`) and FastAPI security scopes on protected endpoints. | **Beta** |
0030: | **FR-09** | The system shall restrict `PUT /api/crops/{crop_id}` and `DELETE /api/crops/{crop_id}` so farmers can only modify their own listings (unless the user has `admin` scope). | **Beta** |
0031: | **FR-10** | The frontend shall provide a User Profile Dashboard displaying user profile details, role badge, and user-owned crop listings. | **Beta** |
0032: 
0033: ---
0034: 
0035: ## 3. Non-Functional Requirements
0036: | Req ID | Category | Specification | Release |
0037: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
0038: | **NFR-01** | **Input Validation** | All API payloads shall be strictly validated using Pydantic v2 models, automatically returning HTTP `422 Unprocessable Entity` on invalid types or negative values. | **MVP** |
0039: | **NFR-02** | **Type Safety** | Frontend components and API responses shall use strict TypeScript interfaces (`strict: true` in `tsconfig.json`). | **MVP** |
0040: | **NFR-03** | **API Performance** | Standard local REST CRUD operations shall respond within 300 ms under normal single-user/demo load. | **MVP** |
0041: | **NFR-04** | **Security & Secrets** | Database credentials (`SUPABASE_URL`, `SUPABASE_KEY`) and JWT secrets shall be stored exclusively in environment variables (`.env` / Vercel Env) and excluded from Git via `.gitignore`. | **MVP** & **Beta** |
0042: | **NFR-05** | **Authorization** | Protected Beta endpoints shall validate JWT signatures and OAuth2 scopes before executing database mutations. | **Beta** |
0043: | **NFR-06** | **Availability** | The Beta release shall deploy cleanly on Vercel with `/api/health` returning `200 OK`. | **Beta** |
0044: 
0045: ---
0046: 
0047: ## 4. External Interface Requirements
0048: - **REST API Interface:** JSON over HTTP (`Content-Type: application/json`) under `/api/*`, documented automatically via OpenAPI/Swagger UI at `/docs`.
0049: - **Database Interface:** PostgreSQL hosted on Supabase accessed via HTTPS PostgREST / SQL driver using environment-configured keys.

0002: **Project Title:** DigitalFarmers

=== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\DigitalFarmers_2331515\frontend\src\api\client.ts ===
0001: let accessToken: string | null = null
0002: let onSessionExpired: (() => void) | null = null
0003: 
0004: export function setAccessToken(token: string | null) {
0005:   accessToken = token
0006: }
0007: 
0008: export function getAccessToken(): string | null {
0009:   return accessToken
0010: }
0011: 
0012: export function setSessionExpiredHandler(handler: () => void) {
0013:   onSessionExpired = handler
0014: }
0015: 
0016: async function refreshAccessToken(): Promise<string | null> {
0017:   const res = await fetch("/api/auth/refresh", {
0018:     method: "POST",
0019:     credentials: "include",
0020:   })
0021:   if (!res.ok) return null
0022:   const data = await res.json()
0023:   accessToken = data.access_token
0024:   return accessToken
0025: }
0026: 
0027: export async function apiFetch(
0028:   path: string,
0029:   options: RequestInit = {},
0030:   retryOn401 = true
0031: ): Promise<Response> {
0032:   const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {})
0033:   if (accessToken) {
0034:     headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${accessToken}`)
0035:   }
0036:   if (options.body && !(options.body instanceof FormData) && !headers.has("Content-Type")) {
0037:     headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json")
0038:   }
0039: 
0040:   let res = await fetch(path, {
0041:     ...options,
0042:     headers,
0043:     credentials: "include",
0044:   })
0045: 
0046:   if (res.status === 401 && retryOn401 && !path.startsWith("/api/auth/login") && !path.startsWith("/api/auth/refresh")) {
0047:     const newToken = await refreshAccessToken()
0048:     if (newToken) {
0049:       headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${newToken}`)
0050:       res = await fetch(path, {
0051:         ...options,
0052:         headers,
0053:         credentials: "include",
0054:       })
0055:     } else if (onSessionExpired) {
0056:       onSessionExpired()
0057:     }
0058:   }
0059: 
0060:   return res
0061: }
0062: 
0063: export async function apiJson<T>(path: string, options: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
0064:   const res = await apiFetch(path, options)
0065:   if (!res.ok) {
0066:     const body = await res.json().catch(() => null)
0067:     throw new Error(body?.detail || `Request failed (${res.status})`)
0068:   }
0069:   return res.json()
0070: }

=== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\DigitalFarmers_2331515\frontend\src\components\ProtectedRoute.tsx ===
0001: import type { ReactNode } from "react"
0002: import { Navigate } from "react-router-dom"
0003: import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext"
0004: import type { UserRole } from "../types/models"
0005: 
0006: export function ProtectedRoute({
0007:   children,
0008:   requiredRole,
0009: }: {
0010:   children: ReactNode
0011:   requiredRole?: UserRole
0012: }) {
0013:   const { user, restoring } = useAuth()
0014: 
0015:   // Prevents flash of guest content while refresh cookie restores the session
0016:   if (restoring) {
0017:     return (
0018:       <div className="mvp-container">
0019:         <div className="panel">Restoring secure session...</div>
0020:       </div>
0021:     )
0022:   }
0023: 
0024:   if (!user) {
0025:     return <Navigate to="/login" replace />
0026:   }
0027: 
0028:   if (requiredRole && user.role !== requiredRole) {
0029:     return <Navigate to="/" replace />
0030:   }
0031: 
0032:   return <>{children}</>
0033: }

=== FILE: C:\Users\ASUS\Downloads\DigitalFarmers_2331515\frontend\src\context\AuthContext.tsx ===
0001: import { createContext, type ReactNode, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react"
0002: import { apiFetch, apiJson, setAccessToken, setSessionExpiredHandler } from "../api/client"
0003: import type { User } from "../types/models"
0004: 
0005: interface AuthContextValue {
0006:   user: User | null
0007:   restoring: boolean
0008:   login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>
0009:   register: (data: { email: string; full_name: string; district: string; password: string }) => Promise<void>
0010:   logout: () => Promise<void>
0011:   reloadUser: () => Promise<void>
0012: }
0013: 
0014: const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | undefined>(undefined)
0015: 
0016: export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
0017:   const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null)
0018:   const [restoring, setRestoring] = useState(true)
0019: 
0020:   useEffect(() => {
0021:     setSessionExpiredHandler(() => {
0022:       setAccessToken(null)
0023:       setUser(null)
0024:     })
0025: 
0026:     async function restoreSession() {
0027:       try {
0028:         const res = await fetch("/api/auth/refresh", {
0029:           method: "POST",
0030:           credentials: "include",
0031:         })
0032:         if (res.ok) {
0033:           const data = await res.json()
0034:           setAccessToken(data.access_token)
0035:           setUser(data.user)
0036:         } else {
0037:           setAccessToken(null)
0038:           setUser(null)
0039:         }
0040:       } catch {
0041:         setAccessToken(null)
0042:         setUser(null)
0043:       } finally {
0044:         setRestoring(false)
0045:       }
0046:     }
0047: 
0048:     void restoreSession()
0049:   }, [])
0050: 
0051:   async function login(email: string, password: string) {
0052:     const data = await apiJson<{ access_token: string; user: User }>("/api/auth/login", {
0053:       method: "POST",
0054:       body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
0055:     })
0056:     setAccessToken(data.access_token)
0057:     setUser(data.user)
0058:   }
0059: 
0060:   async function register(payload: { email: string; full_name: string; district: string; password: string }) {
0061:     const data = await apiJson<{ access_token: string; user: User }>("/api/auth/register", {
0062:       method: "POST",
0063:       body: JSON.stringify(payload),
0064:     })
0065:     setAccessToken(data.access_token)
0066:     setUser(data.user)
0067:   }
0068: 
0069:   async function logout() {
0070:     await apiFetch("/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST" }, false).catch(() => null)
0071:     setAccessToken(null)
0072:     setUser(null)
0073:   }
0074: 
0075:   async function reloadUser() {
0076:     const me = await apiJson<User>("/api/auth/me")
0077:     setUser(me)
0078:   }
0079: 
0080:   return (
0081:     <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, restoring, login, register, logout, reloadUser }}>
0082:       {children}
0083:     </AuthContext.Provider>
0084:   )
0085: }
0086: 
0087: export function useAuth() {
0088:   const ctx = useContext(AuthContext)
0089:   if (!ctx) throw new Error("useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider")
0090:   return ctx
0091: }

0057:     if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
0058:       setError("New password and confirm password do not match.")
0059:       return
0060:     }
0061:     try {
0062:       await apiJson("/api/auth/change-password", {
0063:         method: "POST",
0064:         body: JSON.stringify({ current_password: currentPassword, new_password: newPassword }),
0065:       })
0066:       setCurrentPassword("")
0067:       setNewPassword("")
0068:       setConfirmPassword("")
0069:       setNotice("Password changed successfully.")
0070:     } catch (err) {
0071:       setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Password change failed")
0072:     }
0073:   }
0074: 
0075:   async function handleRevokeSession(sessionId: string) {
0076:     setError("")
0077:     setNotice("")
0078:     try {
0079:       await apiJson(`/api/auth/sessions/${sessionId}`, { method: "DELETE" })
0080:       setNotice("Session revoked.")
0081:       await loadSessions()
0082:     } catch (err) {
0083:       setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Revoke failed")
0084:     }
0085:   }
0086: 
0087:   return (
0088:     <main className="mvp-container">
0089:       {error && <div className="alert error">{error}</div>}
0090:       {notice && <div className="alert success">{notice}</div>}
0091: 
0092:       <section className="panel">
0093:         <div className="panel-header">
0094:           <h2>Profile Details ({user?.email})</h2>

0106:           <div className="form-actions">
0107:             <button type="submit" className="btn-primary">Update Profile</button>
0108:           </div>
0109:         </form>
0110:       </section>
0111: 
0112:       <section className="panel">
0113:         <div className="panel-header">
0114:           <h2>Change Password</h2>
0115:         </div>
0116:         <form className="crop-form" onSubmit={handleChangePassword}>
0117:           <div className="field-group">

0120:           </div>

0122:             <label>New Password (min 8 chars)</label>
0123:             <input type="password" minLength={8} value={newPassword} onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)} required />

0125:           <div className="field-group">
0126:             <label>Confirm New Password</label>
